{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01TsELVkfhvGeZvw8FxihL71","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01Hwy4xzoD3Q4j8gavVJEfSW","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/market_analysis.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization Market Clearing Analysis\nCompares base case vs counterfactual scenario with relaxed transmission constraint\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import linprog\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data from JSON\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef build_susceptance_matrix(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus susceptance matrix (B matrix) for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance (inverse of reactance)\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            B[t_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            B[f_idx, f_idx] += b\n            B[t_idx, t_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_map\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_multiplier=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization\n\n    Parameters:\n    - network: Network data dictionary\n    - line_capacity_multiplier: dict mapping (from_bus, to_bus) to capacity multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n    - Dictionary with optimization results\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Reserve parameters\n    reserve_capacity = network.get('reserve_capacity', {})\n    reserve_requirement = network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Generator information\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Convert to per unit\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7].astype(int)\n\n    n_gens = len(gens)\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Cost coefficients (assuming quadratic: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    # For linear programming, we use c1 (marginal cost)\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])  # 1=piecewise linear, 2=polynomial\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # c1 coefficient (linear term) - convert to $/MWh\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4 + n_cost - 2] * baseMVA\n            elif n_cost == 1:\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4] * baseMVA\n\n    # Reserve cost ($/MW)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0)\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0  # Default reserve cost\n\n    # Load data\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Active power demand in per unit\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus (first slack bus or bus 1)\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables: [Pg (n_gens), theta (n_buses), reserve (n_gens)]\n    n_vars = n_gens + n_buses + n_gens\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost (generation cost + reserve cost)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gens] = gen_cost  # Generation cost\n    c[n_gens + n_buses:] = reserve_cost  # Reserve cost\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus (equality constraint)\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n        # Generation contribution\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        # Network injection (B * theta)\n        row[n_gens:n_gens + n_buses] = -B[bus_idx, :]\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(bus_load[bus_idx])\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    row[n_gens + ref_bus_idx] = 1.0\n    A_eq.append(row)\n    b_eq.append(0.0)\n\n    # 3. Generator capacity limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if not active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        # Pg <= Pmax\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n\n        # -Pg <= -Pmin\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[gen_idx] = -1.0\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(-gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if not active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[gen_idx] = 1.0  # Pg\n        row[n_gens + n_buses + gen_idx] = 1.0  # Reserve\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (thermal limits)\n    for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n        rate = branch[5] / baseMVA  # Thermal limit (RATE_A) in per unit\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10 or rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x  # Susceptance\n\n        # Flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        # Flow <= rate\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[n_gens + f_idx] = b\n        row[n_gens + t_idx] = -b\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(rate)\n\n        # -Flow <= rate (i.e., Flow >= -rate)\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[n_gens + f_idx] = -b\n        row[n_gens + t_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(rate)\n\n    # 6. Total reserve requirement\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            row[n_gens + n_buses + gen_idx] = 1.0\n    A_ub.append(-row)  # Sum of reserves >= reserve_requirement\n    b_ub.append(-reserve_requirement / baseMVA)\n\n    # 7. Reserve non-negativity (handled by bounds)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Pg bounds\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[gen_idx], gen_pmax[gen_idx]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Theta bounds (allow wide range)\n    for _ in range(n_buses):\n        bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            bounds.append((0, gen_pmax[gen_idx]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Convert to numpy arrays\n    A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else np.zeros((0, n_vars))\n    b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else np.zeros(0)\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq)\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq)\n\n    # Solve LP\n    print(f\"Solving LP with {n_vars} variables, {len(A_eq)} equality constraints, {len(A_ub)} inequality constraints\")\n\n    result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,\n                     bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg = result.x[0:n_gens] * baseMVA  # Convert back to MW\n    theta = result.x[n_gens:n_gens + n_buses]\n    reserves = result.x[n_gens + n_buses:] * baseMVA  # Convert back to MW\n\n    # Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance (inverse of reactance)\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            B[t_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            B[f_idx, f_idx] += b\n            B[t_idx, t_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_map\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_multiplier=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization\n\n    Parameters:\n    - network: Network data dictionary\n    - line_capacity_multiplier: dict mapping (from_bus, to_bus) to capacity multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n    - Dictionary with optimization results\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Reserve parameters\n    reserve_capacity = network.get('reserve_capacity', {})\n    reserve_requirement = network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Generator information\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Convert to per unit\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7].astype(int)\n\n    n_gens = len(gens)\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Cost coefficients (assuming quadratic: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    # For linear programming, we use c1 (marginal cost)\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])  # 1=piecewise linear, 2=polynomial\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # c1 coefficient (linear term) - convert to $/MWh\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4 + n_cost - 2] * baseMVA\n            elif n_cost == 1:\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4] * baseMVA\n\n    # Reserve cost ($/MW)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0)\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0  # Default reserve cost\n\n    # Load data\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Active power demand in per unit\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus (first slack bus or bus 1)\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables: [Pg (n_gens), theta (n_buses), reserve (n_gens)]\n    n_vars = n_gens + n_buses + n_gens\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost (generation cost + reserve cost)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gens] = gen_cost  # Generation cost\n    c[n_gens + n_buses:] = reserve_cost  # Reserve cost\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus (equality constraint)\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n        # Generation contribution\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        # Network injection (B * theta)\n        row[n_gens:n_gens + n_buses] = -B[bus_idx, :]\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(bus_load[bus_idx])\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    row[n_gens + ref_bus_idx] = 1.0\n    A_eq.append(row)\n    b_eq.append(0.0)\n\n    # 3. Generator capacity limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if not active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        # Pg <= Pmax\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n\n        # -Pg <= -Pmin\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[gen_idx] = -1.0\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(-gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if not active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[gen_idx] = 1.0  # Pg\n        row[n_gens + n_buses + gen_idx] = 1.0  # Reserve\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (thermal limits)\n    for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n        rate = branch[5] / baseMVA  # Thermal limit (RATE_A) in per unit\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10 or rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x  # Susceptance\n\n        # Flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        # Flow <= rate\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[n_gens + f_idx] = b\n        row[n_gens + t_idx] = -b\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(rate)\n\n        # -Flow <= rate (i.e., Flow >= -rate)\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[n_gens + f_idx] = -b\n        row[n_gens + t_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(rate)\n\n    # 6. Total reserve requirement\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            row[n_gens + n_buses + gen_idx] = 1.0\n    A_ub.append(-row)  # Sum of reserves >= reserve_requirement\n    b_ub.append(-reserve_requirement / baseMVA)\n\n    # 7. Reserve non-negativity (handled by bounds)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Pg bounds\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[gen_idx], gen_pmax[gen_idx]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Theta bounds (allow wide range)\n    for _ in range(n_buses):\n        bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            bounds.append((0, gen_pmax[gen_idx]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Convert to numpy arrays\n    A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else np.zeros((0, n_vars))\n    b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else np.zeros(0)\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq)\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq)\n\n    # Solve LP\n    print(f\"Solving LP with {n_vars} variables, {len(A_eq)} equality constraints, {len(A_ub)} inequality constraints\")\n\n    result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,\n                     bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg = result.x[0:n_gens] * baseMVA  # Convert back to MW\n    theta = result.x[n_gens:n_gens + n_buses]\n    reserves = result.x[n_gens + n_buses:] * baseMVA  # Convert back to MW\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values (shadow prices of power balance constraints)\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    if hasattr(result, 'ineqlin') and hasattr(result, 'eqlin'):\n        # Extract dual values for equality constraints (power balance)\n        dual_eq = result.eqlin.marginals if hasattr(result.eqlin, 'marginals') else None\n        if dual_eq is not None and len(dual_eq) >= n_buses:\n            lmps = -dual_eq[0:n_buses] * baseMVA  # Convert to $/MWh\n\n    # Alternative: calculate LMPs from generator marginal costs if dual not available\n    if np.all(lmps == 0):\n        # Use a simple heuristic: average marginal cost of online generators\n        online_costs = gen_cost[active_gens & (Pg > gen_pmin * baseMVA * 0.99)]\n        if len(online_costs) > 0:\n            avg_lmp = np.mean(online_costs)\n            lmps[:] = avg_lmp\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price (dual of reserve requirement constraint)\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    # Simplified: use minimum reserve cost\n    active_reserve_costs = reserve_cost[active_gens & (reserves > 0.01)]\n    if len(active_reserve_costs) > 0:\n        reserve_mcp = np.min(active_reserve_costs)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5]  # MW\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        actual_rate = rate\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            actual_rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        flow = b * (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx]) * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n        loading = abs(flow) / actual_rate if actual_rate > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from': from_bus,\n            'to': to_bus,\n            'flow_MW': float(flow),\n            'limit_MW': float(actual_rate),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading * 100)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'success': True,\n        'total_cost': float(result.fun),  # $/hour\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'theta': theta,\n        'reserves': reserves,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'bus_map': bus_map,\n        'bus_nums': bus_nums\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {network['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Reserve parameters\n    reserve_capacity = network.get('reserve_capacity', {})\n    reserve_requirement = network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Generator information\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Convert to per unit\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7].astype(int)\n\n    n_gens = len(gens)\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Cost coefficients\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # c1 coefficient (linear term) - convert to $/MWh\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4 + n_cost - 2] * baseMVA\n            elif n_cost == 1:\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4] * baseMVA\n\n    # Reserve cost ($/MW)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0)\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0\n\n    # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Generator power output (per unit)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_buses)  # Bus voltage angles\n    reserve = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Reserve provision (per unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost\n    objective = cp.Minimize(gen_cost @ Pg + reserve_cost @ reserve)\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = cp.sum([\n            Pg[gen_idx] for gen_idx in range(n_gens)\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]\n        ])\n\n        # Network injection\n        net_injection = B[bus_idx, :] @ theta\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load - net_injection = 0\n        power_balance_constraints.append(gen_at_bus - bus_load[bus_idx] - net_injection == 0)\n\n    constraints.extend(power_balance_constraints)\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Generator limits\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] >= gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] + reserve[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] >= 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5] / baseMVA  # Thermal limit in per unit\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10 or rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        flow = b * (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx])\n        constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n        constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n\n    # 6. Total reserve requirement\n    reserve_req_constraint = cp.sum(reserve) >= reserve_requirement / baseMVA\n    constraints.append(reserve_req_constraint)\n\n    # Solve problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    print(f\"Solving CVXPY problem with {n_gens + n_buses + n_gens} variables\")\n\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {problem.status}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg_sol = Pg.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    theta_sol = theta.value\n    reserves_sol = reserve.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values of power balance constraints\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for i, constraint in enumerate(power_balance_constraints[:n_buses]):\n        if constraint.dual_value is not None:\n            # Dual value is negative of LMP, convert to $/MWh\n            lmps[i] = -constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n        else:\n            lmps[i] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    if reserve_req_constraint.dual_value is not None:\n        reserve_mcp = -reserve_req_constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n    else:\n        # $/hour\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'reserves': reserves_sol,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'bus_map': bus_map,\n        'bus_nums': bus_nums\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {network['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Load data\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Active power demand in per unit\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Generator power output (per unit)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_buses)  # Bus voltage angles\n    reserve = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Reserve provision (per unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost\n    objective = cp.Minimize(gen_cost @ Pg + reserve_cost @ reserve)\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = cp.sum([\n            Pg[gen_idx] for gen_idx in range(n_gens)\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]\n        ])\n\n        # Network injection\n        net_injection = B[bus_idx, :] @ theta\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load - net_injection = 0\n        power_balance_constraints.append(gen_at_bus - bus_load[bus_idx] - net_injection == 0)\n\n    constraints.extend(power_balance_constraints)\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Generator limits\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] >= gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] + reserve[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] >= 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5] / baseMVA  # Thermal limit in per unit\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10 or rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        flow = b * (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx])\n        constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n        constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n\n    # 6. Total reserve requirement\n    reserve_req_constraint = cp.sum(reserve) >= reserve_requirement / baseMVA\n    constraints.append(reserve_req_constraint)\n\n    # Solve problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    print(f\"Solving CVXPY problem with {n_gens + n_buses + n_gens} variables\")\n\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {problem.status}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg_sol = Pg.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    theta_sol = theta.value\n    reserves_sol = reserve.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values of power balance constraints\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for i, constraint in enumerate(power_balance_constraints[:n_buses]):\n        if constraint.dual_value is not None:\n            # Dual value is negative of LMP, convert to $/MWh\n            lmps[i] = -constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n        else:\n            lmps[i] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    if reserve_req_constraint.dual_value is not None:\n        reserve_mcp = -reserve_req_constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n    else:\n        # Fallback to minimum reserve cost\n        active_reserve_costs = reserve_cost[active_gens & (reserves_sol > 0.01)]\n        if len(active_reserve_costs) > 0:\n            reserve_mcp = np.min(active_reserve_costs)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5]  # MW\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        actual_rate = rate\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            actual_rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        flow = b * (theta_sol[f_idx] - theta_sol[t_idx]) * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n        loading = abs(flow) / actual_rate if actual_rate > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from': from_bus,\n            'to': to_bus,\n            'flow_MW': float(flow),\n            'limit_MW': float(actual_rate),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading * 100)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'success': True,\n        'total_cost': float(problem.value),  # $/hour\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'reserves': reserves_sol,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'bus_map': bus_map,\n        'bus_nums': bus_nums\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {network['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance (inverse of reactance)\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            B[t_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            B[f_idx, f_idx] += b\n            B[t_idx, t_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_map\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_multiplier=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization using CVXPY\n\n    Parameters:\n    - network: Network data dictionary\n    - line_capacity_multiplier: dict mapping (from_bus, to_bus) to capacity multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n    - Dictionary with optimization results\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Reserve parameters\n    reserve_capacity = network.get('reserve_capacity', {})\n    reserve_requirement = network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Generator information\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Convert to per unit\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7].astype(int)\n\n    n_gens = len(gens)\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Cost coefficients\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # c1 coefficient (linear term) - convert to $/MWh\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4 + n_cost - 2] * baseMVA\n            elif n_cost == 1:\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4] * baseMVA\n\n    # Reserve cost ($/MW)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0)\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0\n\n    # Load data\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Active power demand in per unit\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Generator power output (per unit)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_buses)  # Bus voltage angles\n    reserve = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Reserve provision (per unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost\n    objective = cp.Minimize(gen_cost @ Pg + reserve_cost @ reserve)\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = cp.sum([\n            Pg[gen_idx] for gen_idx in range(n_gens)\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]\n        ])\n\n        # Network injection\n        net_injection = B[bus_idx, :] @ theta\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load - net_injection = 0\n        power_balance_constraints.append(gen_at_bus - bus_load[bus_idx] - net_injection == 0)\n\n    constraints.extend(power_balance_constraints)\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Generator limits\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] >= gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] + reserve[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] >= 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5] / baseMVA  # Thermal limit in per unit\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10 or rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        flow = b * (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx])\n        constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n        constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n\n    # 6. Total reserve requirement\n    reserve_req_constraint = cp.sum(reserve) >= reserve_requirement / baseMVA\n    constraints.append(reserve_req_constraint)\n\n    # Solve problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    print(f\"Solving CVXPY problem with {n_gens + n_buses + n_gens} variables\")\n\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {problem.status}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg_sol = Pg.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    theta_sol = theta.value\n    reserves_sol = reserve.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values of power balance constraints\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for i, constraint in enumerate(power_balance_constraints[:n_buses]):\n        if constraint.dual_value is not None:\n            # Dual value is negative of LMP, convert to $/MWh\n            lmps[i] = -constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n        else:\n            lmps[i] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    if reserve_req_constraint.dual_value is not None:\n        reserve_mcp = -reserve_req_constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n    else:\n        # Fallback to minimum reserve cost\n        active_reserve_costs = reserve_cost[active_gens & (reserves_sol > 0.01)]\n        if len(active_reserve_costs) > 0:\n            reserve_mcp = np.min(active_reserve_costs)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5]  # MW\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        actual_rate = rate\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            actual_rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        flow = b * (theta_sol[f_idx] - theta_sol[t_idx]) * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n        loading = abs(flow) / actual_rate if actual_rate > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from': from_bus,\n            'to': to_bus,\n            'flow_MW': float(flow),\n            'limit_MW': float(actual_rate),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading * 100)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'success': True,\n        'total_cost': float(problem.value),  # $/hour\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'reserves': reserves_sol,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'bus_map': bus_map,\n        'bus_nums': bus_nums\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {network['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Base case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running BASE CASE optimization...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    base_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network)\n\n    if not base_result or not base_result['success']:\n        print(\"Base case optimization failed!\")\n        return\n\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Base case LMP range: ${np.min(base_result['lmps']):.2f} to ${np.max(base_result['lmps']):.2f}/MWh\")\n\n    # Find binding lines in base case\n    binding_lines_base = [\n        bf for bf in base_result['branch_flows']\n        if bf['loading_pct'] >= 99.0\n    ]\n    print(f\"Binding lines in base case: {len(binding_lines_base)}\")\n\n    # Check if line 64->1501 is binding\n    line_64_1501_base = None\n    for bl in binding_lines_base:\n        if bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501:\n            line_64_1501_base = bl\n            print(f\"  *** Line 64->1501: {bl['flow_MW']:.1f} MW / {bl['limit_MW']:.1f} MW ({bl['loading_pct']:.1f}%) ***\")\n\n    for bl in binding_lines_base[:10]:\n        if not (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501):\n            print(f\"  Line {bl['from']}->{bl['to']}: {bl['flow_MW']:.1f} MW / {bl['limit_MW']:.1f} MW ({bl['loading_pct']:.1f}%)\")\n\n    # Analysis\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"IMPACT ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost'] - cf_result['total_cost']\n    print(f\"Cost reduction: ${cost_reduction:,.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # Try different solvers in order of preference\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False)\n    except:\n        try:\n            problem.solve(solver=cp.SCS, verbose=False, eps=1e-5)\n        except:\n            problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {problem.status}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # Reserve parameters\n    reserve_capacity = network.get('reserve_capacity', {})\n    reserve_requirement = network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Generator information\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Convert to per unit\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7].astype(int)\n\n    n_gens = len(gens)\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Cost coefficients (already in $/MWh, not per unit)\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # Divide to get $/pu\n\n    # Reserve cost ($/MW)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0)\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0\n\n    # Load data\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Active power demand in per unit\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Generator power output (per unit)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_buses)  # Bus voltage angles\n    reserve = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Reserve provision (per unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost\n    objective = cp.Minimize(gen_cost @ Pg + reserve_cost @ reserve)\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = cp.sum([\n            Pg[gen_idx] for gen_idx in range(n_gens)\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]\n        ])\n\n        # Network injection\n        net_injection = B[bus_idx, :] @ theta\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load - net_injection = 0\n        power_balance_constraints.append(gen_at_bus - bus_load[bus_idx] - net_injection == 0)\n\n    constraints.extend(power_balance_constraints)\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Generator limits\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] >= gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] + reserve[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] >= 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 5. Total reserve requirement\n    reserve_req_constraint = cp.sum(reserve) >= reserve_requirement / baseMVA\n    constraints.append(reserve_req_constraint)\n\n    # Solve problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    print(f\"Solving CVXPY problem with {n_gens + n_buses + n_gens} variables\")\n\n    # Try different solvers in order of preference\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False)\n    except:\n        try:\n            problem.solve(solver=cp.SCS, verbose=False, eps=1e-5)\n        except:\n            problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {problem.status}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg_sol = Pg.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    theta_sol = theta.value\n    reserves_sol = reserve.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values of power balance constraints\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for i, constraint in enumerate(power_balance_constraints[:n_buses]):\n        if constraint.dual_value is not None:\n            # Dual value is negative of LMP, convert to $/MWh\n            lmps[i] = -constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n        else:\n            lmps[i] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    if reserve_req_constraint.dual_value is not None:\n        reserve_mcp = -reserve_req_constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n    else:\n        # Fallback to minimum reserve cost\n        active_reserve_costs = reserve_cost[active_gens & (reserves_sol > 0.01)]\n        if len(active_reserve_costs) > 0:\n            reserve_mcp = np.min(active_reserve_costs)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5]  # MW\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        actual_rate = rate\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            actual_rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        flow = b * (theta_sol[f_idx] - theta_sol[t_idx]) * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n        loading = abs(flow) / actual_rate if actual_rate > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from': from_bus,\n            'to': to_bus,\n            'flow_MW': float(flow),\n            'limit_MW': float(actual_rate),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading * 100)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'success': True,\n        'total_cost': float(problem.value),  # $/hour\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'reserves': reserves_sol,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'bus_map': bus_map,\n        'bus_nums': bus_nums\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {network['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values of power balance constraints\n    # The dual value gives the marginal value in the objective units ($/pu)\n    # To convert to $/MWh, we need to understand that the dual is dCost/dLoad\n    # Since Load is in per unit and Cost is in $, dual is in $/pu\n    # Therefore LMP in $/MWh = dual * baseMVA\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for i, constraint in enumerate(power_balance_constraints[:n_buses]):\n        if constraint.dual_value is not None:\n            # Power balance: gen - load - net_injection = 0\n            # Dual of this constraint is the LMP (not negative)\n            lmps[i] = constraint.dual_value\n        else:\n            lmps[i] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price (convert from $/pu to $/MW)\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    if reserve_req_constraint.dual_value is not None:\n        reserve_mcp = reserve_req_constraint.dual_value\n    else:\n        # Fallback to minimum reserve cost\n        active_reserve_costs = reserve_cost[active_gens & (reserves_sol > 0.01)] * baseMVA\n        if len(active_reserve_costs) > 0:\n            reserve_mcp = np.min(active_reserve_costs)","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization Market Clearing Analysis using CVXPY\nCompares base case vs counterfactual scenario with relaxed transmission constraint\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\n\ndef load_network(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data from JSON\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef build_susceptance_matrix(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus susceptance matrix (B matrix) for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance (inverse of reactance)\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            B[t_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            B[f_idx, f_idx] += b\n            B[t_idx, t_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_map\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_multiplier=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization using CVXPY\n\n    Parameters:\n    - network: Network data dictionary\n    - line_capacity_multiplier: dict mapping (from_bus, to_bus) to capacity multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n    - Dictionary with optimization results\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Reserve parameters\n    reserve_capacity = network.get('reserve_capacity', {})\n    reserve_requirement = network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Generator information\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Convert to per unit\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7].astype(int)\n\n    n_gens = len(gens)\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Cost coefficients (already in $/MWh, not per unit)\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # c1 coefficient (linear term) - already in $/MWh\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4 + n_cost - 2] / baseMVA  # Divide to get $/pu\n            elif n_cost == 1:\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4] / baseMVA  # Divide to get $/pu\n\n    # Reserve cost (convert from $/MW to $/pu)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0) / baseMVA\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0 / baseMVA\n\n    # Load data\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Active power demand in per unit\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Generator power output (per unit)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_buses)  # Bus voltage angles\n    reserve = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Reserve provision (per unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost\n    objective = cp.Minimize(gen_cost @ Pg + reserve_cost @ reserve)\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = cp.sum([\n            Pg[gen_idx] for gen_idx in range(n_gens)\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]\n        ])\n\n        # Network injection\n        net_injection = B[bus_idx, :] @ theta\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load - net_injection = 0\n        power_balance_constraints.append(gen_at_bus - bus_load[bus_idx] - net_injection == 0)\n\n    constraints.extend(power_balance_constraints)\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Generator limits\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] >= gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] + reserve[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] >= 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5] / baseMVA  # Thermal limit in per unit\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10 or rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        flow = b * (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx])\n        constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n        constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n\n    # 6. Total reserve requirement\n    reserve_req_constraint = cp.sum(reserve) >= reserve_requirement / baseMVA\n    constraints.append(reserve_req_constraint)\n\n    # Solve problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    print(f\"Solving CVXPY problem with {n_gens + n_buses + n_gens} variables\")\n\n    # Try different solvers in order of preference\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False)\n    except:\n        try:\n            problem.solve(solver=cp.SCS, verbose=False, eps=1e-5)\n        except:\n            problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {problem.status}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg_sol = Pg.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    theta_sol = theta.value\n    reserves_sol = reserve.value * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values of power balance constraints\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for i, constraint in enumerate(power_balance_constraints[:n_buses]):\n        if constraint.dual_value is not None:\n            # Dual value is negative of LMP, convert to $/MWh\n            lmps[i] = -constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n        else:\n            lmps[i] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    if reserve_req_constraint.dual_value is not None:\n        reserve_mcp = -reserve_req_constraint.dual_value * baseMVA\n    else:\n        # Fallback to minimum reserve cost\n        active_reserve_costs = reserve_cost[active_gens & (reserves_sol > 0.01)]\n        if len(active_reserve_costs) > 0:\n            reserve_mcp = np.min(active_reserve_costs)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5]  # MW\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        actual_rate = rate\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            actual_rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        flow = b * (theta_sol[f_idx] - theta_sol[t_idx]) * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n\n        loading = abs(flow) / actual_rate if actual_rate > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from': from_bus,\n            'to': to_bus,\n            'flow_MW': float(flow),\n            'limit_MW': float(actual_rate),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading * 100)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'success': True,\n        'total_cost': float(problem.value),  # $/hour\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'reserves': reserves_sol,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'bus_map': bus_map,\n        'bus_nums': bus_nums\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {network['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Base case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running BASE CASE optimization...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    base_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network)\n\n    if not base_result or not base_result['success']:\n        print(\"Base case optimization failed!\")\n        return\n\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Base case LMP range: ${np.min(base_result['lmps']):.2f} to ${np.max(base_result['lmps']):.2f}/MWh\")\n\n    # Find binding lines in base case\n    binding_lines_base = [\n        bf for bf in base_result['branch_flows']\n        if bf['loading_pct'] >= 99.0\n    ]\n    print(f\"Binding lines in base case: {len(binding_lines_base)}\")\n\n    # Check if line 64->1501 is binding\n    line_64_1501_base = None\n    for bl in binding_lines_base:\n        if bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501:\n            line_64_1501_base = bl\n            print(f\"  *** Line 64->1501: {bl['flow_MW']:.1f} MW / {bl['limit_MW']:.1f} MW ({bl['loading_pct']:.1f}%) ***\")\n\n    for bl in binding_lines_base[:10]:\n        if not (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501):\n            print(f\"  Line {bl['from']}->{bl['to']}: {bl['flow_MW']:.1f} MW / {bl['limit_MW']:.1f} MW ({bl['loading_pct']:.1f}%)\")\n\n    # Counterfactual case: increase capacity of line 64->1501 by 20%\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running COUNTERFACTUAL optimization (line 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    line_multiplier = {(64, 1501): 1.20}\n    cf_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_multiplier=line_multiplier)\n\n    if not cf_result or not cf_result['success']:\n        print(\"Counterfactual optimization failed!\")\n        return\n\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual LMP range: ${np.min(cf_result['lmps']):.2f} to ${np.max(cf_result['lmps']):.2f}/MWh\")\n\n    # Find binding lines in counterfactual\n    binding_lines_cf = [\n        bf for bf in cf_result['branch_flows']\n        if bf['loading_pct'] >= 99.0\n    ]\n    print(f\"Binding lines in counterfactual: {len(binding_lines_cf)}\")\n\n    # Check if line 64->1501 is still binding\n    line_64_1501_binding_cf = any(\n        (bf['from'] == 64 and bf['to'] == 1501) for bf in binding_lines_cf\n    )\n\n    # Get the line 64->1501 status in counterfactual\n    for bf in cf_result['branch_flows']:\n        if bf['from'] == 64 and bf['to'] == 1501:\n            print(f\"  Line 64->1501 in counterfactual: {bf['flow_MW']:.1f} MW / {bf['limit_MW']:.1f} MW ({bf['loading_pct']:.1f}%)\")\n            break\n\n    # Analysis\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"IMPACT ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost'] - cf_result['total_cost']\n    print(f\"Cost reduction: ${cost_reduction:,.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # Calculate LMP changes\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for i, bus_num in enumerate(base_result['bus_nums']):\n        base_lmp = base_result['lmps'][i]\n        cf_lmp = cf_result['lmps'][i]\n        delta = cf_lmp - base_lmp\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': int(bus_num),\n            'base_lmp': float(base_lmp),\n            'cf_lmp': float(cf_lmp),\n            'delta': float(delta)\n        })\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drop\n    lmp_changes_sorted = sorted(lmp_changes, key=lambda x: x['delta'])\n    top_3_drops = lmp_changes_sorted[:3]\n\n    print(\"\\nBuses with largest LMP drop:\")\n    for i, drop in enumerate(top_3_drops, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. Bus {drop['bus']}: ${drop['base_lmp']:.2f} -> ${drop['cf_lmp']:.2f} (Δ=${drop['delta']:.2f}/MWh)\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nCongestion relieved on line 64->1501: {not line_64_1501_binding_cf}\")\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Generating report.json...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    report = {\n        \"base_case\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": float(base_result['total_cost']),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": [\n                {\"bus\": int(bus_num), \"lmp_dollars_per_MWh\": float(base_result['lmps'][i])}\n                for i, bus_num in enumerate(base_result['bus_nums'])\n            ],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": float(base_result['reserve_mcp']),\n            \"binding_lines\": [\n                {\"from\": bl['from'], \"to\": bl['to'], \"flow_MW\": bl['flow_MW'], \"limit_MW\": bl['limit_MW']}\n                for bl in binding_lines_base\n            ]\n        },\n        \"counterfactual\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": float(cf_result['total_cost']),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": [\n                {\"bus\": int(bus_num), \"lmp_dollars_per_MWh\": float(cf_result['lmps'][i])}\n                for i, bus_num in enumerate(cf_result['bus_nums'])\n            ],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": float(cf_result['reserve_mcp']),\n            \"binding_lines\": [\n                {\"from\": bl['from'], \"to\": bl['to'], \"flow_MW\": bl['flow_MW'], \"limit_MW\": bl['limit_MW']}\n                for bl in binding_lines_cf\n            ]\n        },\n Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance in per unit\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance (inverse of reactance)\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            b = baseMVA / x  # Convert to MW units\n            B[f_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            B[t_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            B[f_idx, f_idx] += b\n            B[t_idx, t_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_map\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_multiplier=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization using CVXPY\n    All units in MW for better numerical stability\n\n    Parameters:\n    - network: Network data dictionary\n    - line_capacity_multiplier: dict mapping (from_bus, to_bus) to capacity multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n    - Dictionary with optimization results\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Reserve parameters\n    reserve_capacity = network.get('reserve_capacity', {})\n    reserve_requirement = network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Generator information (all in MW)\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]  # MW\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]  # MW\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7].astype(int)\n\n    n_gens = len(gens)\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Cost coefficients ($/MWh)\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # c1 coefficient (linear term) in $/MWh\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4 + n_cost - 2]\n            elif n_cost == 1:\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4]\n\n    # Reserve cost ($/MW)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0)\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0\n\n    # Load data (MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2]  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix (in MW units)\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Decision variables (all in MW)\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Generator power output (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_buses)  # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n    reserve = cp.Variable(n_gens)  # Reserve provision (MW)\n\n    # Objective: minimize total cost ($/hour)\n    objective = cp.Minimize(gen_cost @ Pg + reserve_cost @ reserve)\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = cp.sum([\n            Pg[gen_idx] for gen_idx in range(n_gens)\n            if bus_map[gen_bus[gen_idx]] == bus_idx and active_gens[gen_idx]\n        ])\n\n        # Network injection (B * theta gives flow in MW)\n        net_injection = B[bus_idx, :] @ theta\n\n        # Power balance: generation = load + net_injection\n        power_balance_constraints.append(gen_at_bus == bus_load[bus_idx] + net_injection)\n\n    constraints.extend(power_balance_constraints)\n\n    # 2. Reference bus angle = 0\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Generator limits\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] >= gen_pmin[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 4. Reserve + Generation <= Pmax (capacity coupling)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if active_gens[gen_idx]:\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] + reserve[gen_idx] <= gen_pmax[gen_idx])\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] >= 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(reserve[gen_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance (pu)\n        rate = branch[5]  # Thermal limit (MW)\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10 or rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = baseMVA / x  # Susceptance in MW units\n\n        # Flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to) in MW\n        flow = b * (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx])\n        constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n        constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n\n    # 6. Total reserve requirement\n    reserve_req_constraint = cp.sum(reserve) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_req_constraint)\n\n    # Solve problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    print(f\"Solving DC-OPF with {n_gens + n_buses + n_gens} variables, {len(constraints)} constraints\")\n\n    # Try different solvers\n    solved = False\n    for solver_name in [cp.ECOS, cp.SCS, cp.CLARABEL]:\n        try:\n            if solver_name == cp.SCS:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver_name, verbose=False, eps=1e-4, max_iters=5000)\n            else:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver_name, verbose=False)\n\n            if problem.status in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n                print(f\"Solved with {solver_name}\")\n                solved = True\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Solver {solver_name} failed: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    if not solved or problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {problem.status}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Pg_sol = Pg.value  # MW\n    theta_sol = theta.value  # radians\n    reserves_sol = reserve.value  # MW\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from dual values of power balance constraints\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for i, constraint in enumerate(power_balance_constraints[:n_buses]):\n        if constraint.dual_value is not None:\n            # Dual value is the marginal cost of serving 1 more MW at this bus\n            lmps[i] = constraint.dual_value\n        else:\n            lmps[i] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    if reserve_req_constraint.dual_value is not None:\n        reserve_mcp = reserve_req_constraint.dual_value\n    else:\n        active_reserve_costs = reserve_cost[active_gens & (reserves_sol > 0.01)]\n        if len(active_reserve_costs) > 0:\n            reserve_mcp = np.min(active_reserve_costs)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n        rate = branch[5]  # MW\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        # Apply capacity multiplier if specified\n        actual_rate = rate\n        if line_capacity_multiplier and (from_bus, to_bus) in line_capacity_multiplier:\n            actual_rate *= line_capacity_multiplier[(from_bus, to_bus)]\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        b = baseMVA / x\n        flow = b * (theta_sol[f_idx] - theta_sol[t_idx])  # MW\n\n        loading = abs(flow) / actual_rate if actual_rate > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from': from_bus,\n            'to': to_bus,\n            'flow_MW': float(flow),\n            'limit_MW': float(actual_rate),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading * 100)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'success': True,\n        'total_cost': float(problem.value),  # $/hour\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'reserves': reserves_sol,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'bus_map': bus_map,\n        'bus_nums': bus_nums\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {network['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {network.get('reserve_requirement', 0)} MW\\n\")\n\n    # Get bus numbers and create mapping\n    bus_nums = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    n_buses = len(bus_nums)\n    bus_map = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_nums)}\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branches:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance in per unit\n\n        if from_bus not in bus_map or to_bus not in bus_map:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = bus_map[from_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_map[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance (inverse of reactance)\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            b = baseMVA / x  # Convert to MW units\n            B[f_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            B[t_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            B[f_idx, f_idx] += b\n            B[t_idx, t_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_map\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_multiplier=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization using CVXPY\n    All units in MW for better numerical stability\n\n    Parameters:\n    - network: Network data dictionary\n    - line_capacity_multiplier: dict mapping (from_bus, to_bus) to capacity multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n    - Dictionary with optimization results\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Cost coefficients ($/MWh)\n    gen_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, cost_data in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(cost_data[0])\n        if model == 2:\n            n_cost = int(cost_data[3])\n            if n_cost >= 2:\n                # c1 coefficient (linear term) in $/MWh\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4 + n_cost - 2]\n            elif n_cost == 1:\n                gen_cost[i] = cost_data[4]\n\n    # Reserve cost ($/MW)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(range(n_gens)):\n        gen_id = str(gen_idx + 1)\n        if gen_id in reserve_capacity:\n            reserve_cost[i] = reserve_capacity[gen_id].get('cost', 5.0)\n        else:\n            reserve_cost[i] = 5.0\n\n    # Load data (MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2]  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix (in MW units)\n    B, _ = build_susceptance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Select reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if int(bus[1]) == 3:  #
